| spec/pap-1.0/08-conversations.md | 8.5 Reading events | `conversations.cursor-opaque` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST NOT · agent — The Personal Agent does not compare or sort cursors. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/spec | Cursors are opaque, so the Personal Agent MUST NOT compare or sort them. - wait is a whole number of seconds, 0 or more. | VERIFIED (1.00) |
| spec/pap-1.0/08-conversations.md | 8.5 Reading events | `conversations.wait-cap` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST · company — wait is a whole number of seconds, 0 or more; the Company holds the request until there is an event, until working changes from true to false, or until wait seconds, and a larger wait than its cap is held only as long as the cap, not rejected. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/spec | The Company holds the request for up to wait seconds until there is an event to return, or until working changes from true to false (8.7). | VERIFIED (0.61) |
| spec/pap-1.0/08-conversations.md | 8.5 Reading events | `conversations.read-fields` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST · company — Every successful read includes conversation_id, events, cursor, has_more, working, handoff, responder and closed, with events empty when there are none. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/spec | A read that times out returns an empty events list. - Every successful read MUST include conversation_id, events, cursor, has_more, and the state fields working, handoff, responder, and closed (8.7), with events empty when there are none. cursor is the last event returned, or the request's cursor if there were none. | VERIFIED (0.87) |
| spec/pap-1.0/08-conversations.md | 8.5 Reading events | `conversations.apply-order` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST · agent — The Personal Agent applies events in order, relies on the state fields once has_more is false, and skips event IDs it has already handled. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/spec | The Personal Agent applies events in order, and relies on the state fields once has_more is false. - The Personal Agent handles events in order and saves the cursor after them. | VERIFIED (0.73) |
| spec/pap-1.0/08-conversations.md | 8.5 Reading events | `conversations.cursor-expired` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST · company — A cursor for events the Company no longer has returns cursor_expired. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/spec | A cursor for events it no longer has returns cursor_expired. | VERIFIED (0.83) |
| spec/pap-1.0/08-conversations.md | 8.6 Streaming | `conversations.stream` | (not in pap-0.3) | MAY · company — A Company may stream events with server-sent events when the read sends Accept: text/event-stream, and a Company that does not stream returns JSON. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/spec | A Company MAY stream events with server-sent events ([SSE](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/server-sent-events.html)). | UNVERIFIED (0.42) |
| spec/pap-1.0/08-conversations.md | 8.6 Streaming | `conversations.working-event` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST · company — In a stream, working is sent as a working event with no SSE id, when the stream opens and each time it changes. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/spec | Changes to handoff, responder, and closed arrive as state events. working isn't an event: the Company sends it as a working event with no SSE id, when the stream opens and each time it changes: | VERIFIED (0.88) |
| spec/pap-1.0/08-conversations.md | 8.6 Streaming | `conversations.stream-close` | (not in pap-0.3) | MAY · company — The Company may close a stream at any time. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/spec | The Company MAY close a stream at any time, for example when the access token expires. | VERIFIED (1.00) |
| spec/pap-1.0/08-conversations.md | 8.6 Streaming | `conversations.last-event-id` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST · both — On reconnect, Last-Event-ID is the last event the Personal Agent handled, and if a request has both Last-Event-ID and cursor, Last-Event-ID wins. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/spec | The Company sets it when it records the message as an event (8.5). \| \| as_of_request \| No \| On a message from the Company Agent, the ID of the latest request it took into account (below). \| \| as_of_cursor \| No \| On a message the Personal Agent wrote, the last cursor it had read (below). \| \| author \| Yes \| Who wrote the message. type is agent if the Personal Agent wrote it, human if the | VERIFIED (0.60) |
| spec/pap-1.0/08-conversations.md | 8.6 Streaming | `conversations.text-delta` | (not in pap-0.3) | MAY · company — While the Company Agent writes a message, the Company may send text-delta events with no SSE id. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/spec | While the Company Agent writes a message, the Company MAY send pieces of its text as text-delta events, with no SSE id: | VERIFIED (1.00) |
| spec/pap-1.0/08-conversations.md | 8.6 Streaming | `conversations.skip-sse` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST · agent — Personal Agents skip stream events with an SSE event name they do not recognize. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/spec | Personal Agents MUST skip stream events with an SSE event name they don't recognize, so later versions and extensions can add them. | VERIFIED (0.89) |
| spec/pap-1.0/08-conversations.md | 8.7 State | `conversations.state-fields` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST · both — working, handoff, responder and closed are independent; a new conversation starts with handoff none, responder agent and closed false. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/spec | A new conversation starts with handoff none, responder agent, and closed false. | VERIFIED (0.73) |
| spec/pap-1.0/08-conversations.md | 8.7 State | `conversations.unknown-state` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST · agent — A Personal Agent treats a handoff it does not recognize as pending, and a responder it does not recognize as human. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/spec | A Personal Agent treats a handoff it doesn't recognize as pending, and a responder it doesn't recognize as human. | VERIFIED (1.00) |
| spec/pap-1.0/08-conversations.md | 8.7 State | `conversations.state-event` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST · company — Every change to handoff, responder or closed adds a state event with all three, and changes to working add no event. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/spec | Every change to handoff, responder, or closed adds a state event with all three, even if there is no new text. | VERIFIED (0.75) |
| spec/pap-1.0/08-conversations.md | 8.7 State | `conversations.message-author-type` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST · company — Each Company message includes author.type, with agent or human. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/spec | The Company sets it when it records the message as an event (8.5). \| \| as_of_request \| No \| On a message from the Company Agent, the ID of the latest request it took into account (below). \| \| as_of_cursor \| No \| On a message the Personal Agent wrote, the last cursor it had read (below). \| \| author \| Yes \| Who wrote the message. type is agent if the Personal Agent wrote it, human if the | VERIFIED (0.86) |
| spec/pap-1.0/08-conversations.md | 8.8 Human or AI | `conversations.author` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST · agent — The Personal Agent sets each message's author.type to agent when it wrote the message, and to human only when the message carries the User's own words; a summary, paraphrase or translation is agent. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/spec | On the Personal Agent's side, the Personal Agent MUST set each message's author.type: agent when the Personal Agent wrote it, and human only when it carries the User's own words. | VERIFIED (0.71) |

| spec/pap-1.0/08-conversations.md | 8.9 Handoff to a person | `conversations.handoff-noop` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST · company — If handoff is already pending, or a person is already handling the conversation, the request changes nothing. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/spec | If handoff is already pending, or a person is already handling the conversation, the request changes nothing. | VERIFIED (0.90) |
| spec/pap-1.0/08-conversations.md | 8.9 Handoff to a person | `conversations.handoff-cancel` | (not in pap-0.3) | MAY · company — On a cancel, the Company may stop looking for a person, or may keep looking. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/guides | Leave out disclosures that read like promotion rather than a notice. - With a company agent, skip greetings and small talk, and keep text short ([8.8](/docs/1.0/spec#human-or-ai)). - Before asking for a person, send a short message that sums up the problem, so they don't have to read the whole conversation ([8.9](/docs/1.0/spec#handoff)). - If the company agent solves the problem while you wait fo | VERIFIED (0.67) |
| spec/pap-1.0/08-conversations.md | 8.9 Handoff to a person | `conversations.handoff-cancel-effect` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST NOT · agent — The Personal Agent does not depend on the cancel taking effect. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/spec | The Personal Agent learns which happened from handoff and responder, and MUST NOT depend on the cancel taking effect. | VERIFIED (1.00) |
| spec/pap-1.0/08-conversations.md | 8.9 Handoff to a person | `conversations.handoff-cancel-noop` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST · company — A cancel when handoff is not pending changes nothing. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/spec | A cancel when handoff isn't pending changes nothing. | VERIFIED (0.83) |
| spec/pap-1.0/08-conversations.md | 8.10 Direct Conversations | `conversations.direct-privacy` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST NOT · company — The Company does not send information in a Direct Conversation that it cannot share with the Personal Agent. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/spec | The Company MUST NOT send information there that it cannot share with the Personal Agent. | VERIFIED (0.78) |
| spec/pap-1.0/08-conversations.md | 8.10 Direct Conversations | `conversations.direct-parent` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST · company — The parent conversation is open, belongs to the same owner, and is not itself a Direct Conversation; otherwise the Company returns conversation_not_found, conversation_closed, invalid_request or direct_conversation_open as section 8.10 specifies. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/spec | Otherwise the Company returns conversation_not_found for an unknown parent or one with a different owner, conversation_closed for a closed one, invalid_request for one that is a Direct Conversation, and direct_conversation_open for one that already has an open Direct Conversation. | VERIFIED (0.71) |
| spec/pap-1.0/08-conversations.md | 8.10 Direct Conversations | `conversations.user-requested-state` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST · both — A user_requested event does not change the conversation's state. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/spec | A new conversation starts with its first message, except when accepting a user_requested event without a message (8.10): | VERIFIED (0.57) |
| spec/pap-1.0/08-conversations.md | 8.10 Direct Conversations | `conversations.user-requested-id` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST · company — The Company rejects a user_requested_event_id that does not name a user_requested event in that parent with invalid_request. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/spec | The Company MUST reject a user_requested_event_id that doesn't name a user_requested event in that parent with invalid_request. | VERIFIED (0.89) |
| spec/pap-1.0/08-conversations.md | 8.10 Direct Conversations | `conversations.direct-omit-message` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST · both — A request without message is valid only when starting a Direct Conversation with both parent_conversation_id and user_requested_event_id. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/spec | A request without message is valid only when starting a Direct Conversation with both IDs. | VERIFIED (0.67) |
| spec/pap-1.0/08-conversations.md | 8.10 Direct Conversations | `conversations.direct-retry` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST · company — Repeating the same parent ID and event ID returns the same Direct Conversation without adding another message, even after it closes. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/spec | Repeating the same parent ID and event ID returns the same Direct Conversation without adding another message, even after it closes. | VERIFIED (0.93) |
| spec/pap-1.0/08-conversations.md | 8.10 Direct Conversations | `conversations.direct-parent-lock` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST · company — While a Direct Conversation is open, messages, handoff requests and handoff cancels to the parent return direct_conversation_open; closing the parent also closes its open Direct Conversation. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/spec | While it is open, the parent can still be read, but messages, handoff requests, and handoff cancels to the parent return direct_conversation_open, with the Direct Conversation's ID in conversation_id. | VERIFIED (0.77) |
| spec/pap-1.0/08-conversations.md | 8.11 Sign-in during a conversation | `conversations.authorization-event` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST · company — When the Company Agent needs the User's account, it adds an authorization event whose error is insufficient_scope and whose scope names the scopes needed. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/spec | When the Company Agent needs the User's account, the Company adds an authorization event: | UNVERIFIED (0.53) |
| spec/pap-1.0/08-conversations.md | 8.11 Sign-in during a conversation | `conversations.authorization-header` | (not in pap-0.3) | MAY · company — When a response carrying an authorization event has not yet sent its headers, the Company may also include the matching WWW-Authenticate challenge. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/spec | When a response carrying an authorization event has not yet sent its headers, the Company MAY also include the matching WWW-Authenticate challenge on the successful response, as [RFC 9110](https://www.rfc-editor.org/rfc/rfc9110#section-11.6.1) §11.6.1 allows. | VERIFIED (1.00) |
| spec/pap-1.0/08-conversations.md | 8.11 Sign-in during a conversation | `conversations.authorization-once` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST · agent — The Personal Agent handles the event and a matching challenge as one authorization need, and does not resubmit an accepted message because it received the challenge. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/spec | The Personal Agent MUST handle the event and matching challenge as one authorization need, and MUST NOT resubmit an accepted message because it received the challenge. | VERIFIED (0.92) |
| spec/pap-1.0/08-conversations.md | 8.11 Sign-in during a conversation | `conversations.token-changes` | (not in pap-0.3) | MAY · agent — The Personal Agent may start or stop sending an access token partway through a conversation, or continue it in a new Session, as long as the request matches the conversation's owner. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/spec | The Personal Agent MAY start or stop sending an access token partway through a conversation, after signing in, refreshing, or signing out, or continue it in a new Session, as long as the request matches the conversation's owner (8.2). | VERIFIED (1.00) |
| spec/pap-1.0/08-conversations.md | 8.11 Sign-in during a conversation | `conversations.account-challenge` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST · company — Once a conversation has used the User's account, a request on it for the same User without an access token for that account gets a challenge. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/spec | Once a conversation has used the User's account, a request on it for the same User without an access token for that account gets a challenge, as an account action does (section 7). | VERIFIED (0.92) |
| spec/pap-1.0/08-conversations.md | 8.12 Closing | `conversations.close-drop` | (not in pap-0.3) | MAY · company — A close does not wait its turn; the Company may act on any request it has not acted on yet, or may drop it. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/spec | Taking a request into account doesn't mean the Company acted on it, because a close can drop requests (8.12). - as_of_cursor: a message the Personal Agent wrote, with author.type agent, MAY include the cursor of the last read it had handled when it wrote the message (8.5), if it has one. | VERIFIED (0.71) |
| spec/pap-1.0/08-conversations.md | 8.12 Closing | `conversations.close-no-depend` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST NOT · agent — The Personal Agent does not depend on either of those outcomes. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/spec | The Company and the Personal Agent MUST NOT depend on either field being present, and treat each only as a hint for interpreting the message. | VERIFIED (0.80) |
| spec/pap-1.0/08-conversations.md | 8.12 Closing | `conversations.closed` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST · company — A closed conversation takes no more messages, handoff requests or handoff cancels: they return conversation_closed; a close sent to a conversation that is already closed returns HTTP 202 and changes nothing. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/spec | A closed conversation takes no more messages, handoff requests, or handoff cancels: they return conversation_closed. | UNVERIFIED (0.53) |
| spec/pap-1.0/08-conversations.md | 8.13 Errors | `conversations.errors` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST · both — Conversation errors use the section 8.13 table: conversation_not_found 404, conversation_closed 409, direct_conversation_open 409, request_id_conflict 409, invalid_cursor 400, cursor_expired 410, invalid_request 400, extension_required 403 and version_mismatch 409. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/spec | All return the same error. \| \| conversation_closed \| 409 \| A message, handoff request, or handoff cancel was sent to a closed conversation (8.12). \| \| direct_conversation_open \| 409 \| The conversation has an open Direct Conversation, named in conversation_id (8.10). \| \| request_id_conflict \| 409 \| A request reused an ID that the Company accepted with a different path or body (8.3). \| \| in | VERIFIED (0.72) |

| spec/pap-1.0/09-tasks.md | 9 Task coverage | `tasks.no-token` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST · agent — The Personal Agent sends GET {tasks.endpoint} with the Session header and no access token. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/spec | The Personal Agent sends GET {tasks.endpoint} with the Session header (5.2) and no access token, and the Company responds with HTTP 200 and a JSON object: | VERIFIED (1.00) |
| spec/pap-1.0/09-tasks.md | 9 Task coverage | `tasks.per-session` | (not in pap-0.3) | MAY · company — A Company may give different Sessions different responses, and verifies the Session header before it does. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/spec | A Company that gives every Session the same response can serve it without verifying the header. - A Company that gives different Sessions different responses MUST mark them private or no-store in Cache-Control ([RFC 9111](https://www.rfc-editor.org/rfc/rfc9111) §5.2.2), so that a shared cache doesn't give one Session's response to another. - A Personal Agent SHOULD get the response before it | VERIFIED (0.89) |
| spec/pap-1.0/09-tasks.md | 9 Task coverage | `tasks.private-cache` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST · company — A Company that gives different Sessions different responses marks them private or no-store in Cache-Control. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/spec | A Company that gives every Session the same response can serve it without verifying the header. - A Company that gives different Sessions different responses MUST mark them private or no-store in Cache-Control ([RFC 9111](https://www.rfc-editor.org/rfc/rfc9111) §5.2.2), so that a shared cache doesn't give one Session's response to another. - A Personal Agent SHOULD get the response before it | VERIFIED (0.90) |
| spec/pap-1.0/09-tasks.md | 9 Task coverage | `tasks.fetch-early` | (not in pap-0.3) | SHOULD · agent — A Personal Agent gets the tasks response before it starts on a task in a Session. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/spec | A Company that gives every Session the same response can serve it without verifying the header. - A Company that gives different Sessions different responses MUST mark them private or no-store in Cache-Control ([RFC 9111](https://www.rfc-editor.org/rfc/rfc9111) §5.2.2), so that a shared cache doesn't give one Session's response to another. - A Personal Agent SHOULD get the response before it | VERIFIED (0.78) |
| spec/pap-1.0/09-tasks.md | 9 Task coverage | `tasks.reuse` | (not in pap-0.3) | MAY · agent — A Personal Agent may reuse the response for the rest of the Session, as its caching headers allow. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/spec | It MAY reuse it for the rest of the Session, as its caching headers allow, and MUST NOT use it in another Session. - Session errors are invalid_session and session_ended (5.3), and version errors version_unsupported (3.4). | VERIFIED (0.67) |
| spec/pap-1.0/09-tasks.md | 9 Task coverage | `tasks.other-session` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST NOT · agent — A Personal Agent does not use a tasks response in another Session. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/spec | A Company that gives every Session the same response can serve it without verifying the header. - A Company that gives different Sessions different responses MUST mark them private or no-store in Cache-Control ([RFC 9111](https://www.rfc-editor.org/rfc/rfc9111) §5.2.2), so that a shared cache doesn't give one Session's response to another. - A Personal Agent SHOULD get the response before it | VERIFIED (0.83) |
| spec/pap-1.0/09-tasks.md | 9 Task coverage | `tasks.missing` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST · agent — When it gets no response, the Personal Agent carries on as if the Company listed no excluded tasks, published no skills and took no feedback. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/spec | When it gets no response, the Personal Agent carries on as if the Company listed no excluded tasks, published no skills, and took no feedback. | VERIFIED (1.00) |
| spec/pap-1.0/09-tasks.md | 9.1 Excluded tasks | `tasks.excluded-shape` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST · company — Each excluded task has a short task phrase and an optional alternative. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/spec | \| Field \| Req. \| Description \| \| --- \| --- \| --- \| \| task \| Yes \| The task, in a short phrase, such as "Send a wire transfer" or "Close an account". \| \| alternative \| No \| Another way to do the task, such as in the Company's app or on its website, or a different task that serves the same need (9.2). \| | VERIFIED (0.71) |
| spec/pap-1.0/09-tasks.md | 9.1 Excluded tasks | `tasks.not-a-limit` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST NOT · company — A limit that depends on the User's account is not an excluded task; the Company returns an error for it. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/spec | - A listed task isn't offered through any of the Company's interfaces: its Company Agent, its APIs, or its website in a browser session. - Whether the User's task is one listed is the Personal Agent's judgment, since each task is a phrase. - A limit that depends on the User's account, such as a transfer limit, isn't an excluded task. | VERIFIED (0.78) |
| spec/pap-1.0/09-tasks.md | 9.2 Alternatives | `tasks.web-no-credentials` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST NOT · agent — A web reference does not authorize sending a Session JWT or access token to its target. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/spec | Personal Agents MUST ignore one that doesn't. - A web reference doesn't authorize sending a Session JWT (5.2) or access token (4.5) to its target. | VERIFIED (0.88) |
| spec/pap-1.0/09-tasks.md | 9.2 Alternatives | `tasks.skip-unknown-ref` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST · agent — Personal Agents skip reference types they do not recognize. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/spec | Acting at another Company uses that Company's discovery, Session, and any needed account authorization. - Personal Agents MUST skip reference types they don't recognize, so later versions can add types, such as the Company Agent or an extension. - An alternative is content from the Company (10.1). | VERIFIED (1.00) |
| spec/pap-1.0/09-tasks.md | 9.2 Alternatives | `tasks.alternative-content` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST · agent — An alternative is content from the Company: its instructions are not instructions the Personal Agent has to follow. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/spec | Acting at another Company uses that Company's discovery, Session, and any needed account authorization. - Personal Agents MUST skip reference types they don't recognize, so later versions can add types, such as the Company Agent or an extension. - An alternative is content from the Company (10.1). | VERIFIED (0.71) |

| spec/pap-1.0/09-tasks.md | 9.3 Feedback | `tasks.feedback-shape` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST · both — A report has task of at most 200 characters and description of at most 2,000 characters, and the description has no identifiers. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/spec | At most 200 characters. \| \| description \| Yes \| What the Personal Agent was trying to accomplish, in detail, and why, without identifiers such as order numbers, account numbers, or names. | VERIFIED (0.57) |
| spec/pap-1.0/09-tasks.md | 9.3 Feedback | `tasks.feedback-verify` | (not in pap-0.3) | SHOULD · company — A Company verifies the Session header on a report, and needs no scope for it. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/spec | - A Company SHOULD verify the Session header on a report, signed in or signed out, and needs no scope for it. | VERIFIED (0.86) |
| spec/pap-1.0/09-tasks.md | 9.3 Feedback | `tasks.feedback-attribute` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST · company — The Company verifies the header before attributing a report to a Personal Agent, User or Session. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/spec | The Company MUST verify the header before attributing a report to a Personal Agent, User, or Session. | VERIFIED (0.90) |
| spec/pap-1.0/09-tasks.md | 9.3 Feedback | `tasks.feedback-no-personal` | (not in pap-0.3) | SHOULD NOT · agent — Personal Agents do not put personal data in a report. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/spec | Defaults to none. \| \| skill_index \| No \| An Agent Skills discovery index of the skills the Company publishes (9.4). \| \| feedback \| No \| endpoint is an HTTPS URL where Personal Agents report a User's task that can't be done (9.3). \| | VERIFIED (0.80) |
| spec/pap-1.0/09-tasks.md | 9.3 Feedback | `tasks.feedback-no-secrets` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST NOT · agent — Personal Agents do not put secrets in a report. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/spec | Personal Agents MUST NOT put secrets in a report (10.2). - A Personal Agent SHOULD send one report for each task it couldn't do, not one for each attempt. - A Personal Agent SHOULD NOT report a task the Company lists as excluded (9.1), since the Company already knows it doesn't offer it. - A report can be wrong, or written to mislead. | VERIFIED (1.00) |
| spec/pap-1.0/09-tasks.md | 9.3 Feedback | `tasks.feedback-once` | (not in pap-0.3) | SHOULD · agent — A Personal Agent sends one report for each task it could not do, not one for each attempt. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/spec | Personal Agents MUST NOT put secrets in a report (10.2). - A Personal Agent SHOULD send one report for each task it couldn't do, not one for each attempt. - A Personal Agent SHOULD NOT report a task the Company lists as excluded (9.1), since the Company already knows it doesn't offer it. - A report can be wrong, or written to mislead. | VERIFIED (0.71) |
| spec/pap-1.0/09-tasks.md | 9.3 Feedback | `tasks.feedback-excluded` | (not in pap-0.3) | SHOULD NOT · agent — A Personal Agent does not report a task the Company lists as excluded. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/spec | Personal Agents MUST NOT put secrets in a report (10.2). - A Personal Agent SHOULD send one report for each task it couldn't do, not one for each attempt. - A Personal Agent SHOULD NOT report a task the Company lists as excluded (9.1), since the Company already knows it doesn't offer it. - A report can be wrong, or written to mislead. | VERIFIED (1.00) |
| spec/pap-1.0/09-tasks.md | 9.3 Feedback | `tasks.feedback-data` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST · company — Companies treat a report's text as data, not as instructions. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/spec | Companies MUST treat a report's text as data, not as instructions, including when a model reads it. | VERIFIED (0.86) |
| spec/pap-1.0/09-tasks.md | 9.3 Feedback | `tasks.feedback-response` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST · company — The Company responds with HTTP 202 and no body, or, when the task can be done after all, with HTTP 200 and an alternative. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/spec | When the task can be done after all, it MAY instead respond with HTTP 200 and an alternative (9.2) as the body, such as the one in 9.2 for paying with a gift card. | VERIFIED (0.75) |
| spec/pap-1.0/09-tasks.md | 9.3 Feedback | `tasks.feedback-accept` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST · agent — Personal Agents accept either feedback response. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/spec | Personal Agents MUST accept either response. | VERIFIED (0.71) |
| spec/pap-1.0/09-tasks.md | 9.3 Feedback | `tasks.feedback-invalid` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST · company — A malformed or too-long report gets invalid_request (HTTP 400). | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/spec | Other errors have a JSON body with an error code, as in 8.13: invalid_request (400) for a report that is malformed or too long. | VERIFIED (0.63) |
| spec/pap-1.0/09-tasks.md | 9.4 Agent context | `tasks.skills` | (not in pap-0.3) | MAY · company — A Company may include skill_index in its tasks response. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/spec | A Company MAY include skill_index in its tasks response (section 9) to publish skills for Personal Agents acting on the User's behalf at that Company. | VERIFIED (1.00) |
| spec/pap-1.0/09-tasks.md | 9.4 Agent context | `tasks.skill-format` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST · company — Companies that publish skill_index follow the pinned Agent Skills discovery revision's index format, artifact distribution, integrity and HTTP requirements for artifacts, with the index in the tasks response instead of fetched from its own URL. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/spec | Companies that publish skill_index MUST follow that revision's index format, artifact distribution, integrity, and HTTP requirements for artifacts, with the index in the tasks response instead of fetched from its own URL. | VERIFIED (0.77) |
| spec/pap-1.0/09-tasks.md | 9.4 Agent context | `tasks.skill-verify` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST · agent — Personal Agents that use the index follow its verification and archive safety requirements before using a skill. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/spec | Personal Agents that use the index MUST follow its verification and archive safety requirements before using a skill. | VERIFIED (0.92) |
| spec/pap-1.0/09-tasks.md | 9.4 Agent context | `tasks.skill-ignore` | (not in pap-0.3) | MAY · agent — Personal Agents may ignore skill_index and use the Company's channels directly. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/spec | Personal Agents MAY ignore skill_index and use those channels directly; a Company does not need to publish skills to support Poppy. | VERIFIED (0.89) |
| spec/pap-1.0/09-tasks.md | 9.4 Agent context | `tasks.skill-use` | (not in pap-0.3) | MAY · agent — Personal Agents may use a skill's descriptions and workflows to carry out the User's task. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/spec | Personal Agents MAY use its descriptions and workflows to carry out the User's task. | VERIFIED (0.78) |
| spec/pap-1.0/09-tasks.md | 9.4 Agent context | `tasks.skill-resolve` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST · agent — Personal Agents resolve an entry's url against tasks.endpoint using RFC 3986 §5, and resolved URLs and redirects still follow section 3's HTTPS and address rules. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/spec | **URLs.** Personal Agents MUST resolve an entry's url against tasks.endpoint, using [RFC 3986 §5](https://www.rfc-editor.org/rfc/rfc3986#section-5). | VERIFIED (0.63) |
| spec/pap-1.0/09-tasks.md | 9.4 Agent context | `tasks.skill-no-credentials` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST NOT · agent — Listing an artifact on another domain does not authorize sending it credentials or Session headers. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/spec | Listing an artifact on another domain does not make that domain an API endpoint or authorize sending it credentials or Session headers. | VERIFIED (0.90) |
| spec/pap-1.0/09-tasks.md | 9.4 Agent context | `tasks.skill-unknown` | (not in pap-0.3) | SHOULD · agent — Personal Agents ignore unknown index fields, skip entries with an unknown type, and ignore the index when $schema is absent or unknown. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/spec | **Compatibility.** Personal Agents SHOULD ignore unknown index fields, and SHOULD skip entries with an unknown type. | VERIFIED (0.75) |
| spec/pap-1.0/09-tasks.md | 9.4 Agent context | `tasks.skill-well-known` | (not in pap-0.3) | MAY · company — A Company may also publish the same index at /.well-known/agent-skills/index.json. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/spec | A Company MAY also publish the same index at /.well-known/agent-skills/index.json for other clients. | VERIFIED (1.00) |
| spec/pap-1.0/09-tasks.md | 9.4 Agent context | `tasks.skill-no-scripts` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST NOT · agent — Personal Agents do not execute scripts from a skill unless the User or the Personal Agent's configuration explicitly allows it. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/spec | Personal Agents MUST NOT execute scripts from a skill unless the User or the Personal Agent's configuration explicitly allows it. | VERIFIED (1.00) |
| spec/pap-1.0/09-tasks.md | 9.4 Agent context | `tasks.skill-no-grant` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST · both — A skill grants no account access, changes no OAuth scopes, and supplies no User approval. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/spec | A skill grants no account access, changes no OAuth scopes, and supplies no User approval. | VERIFIED (0.91) |
| spec/pap-1.0/10-security.md | 10.1 Content from Companies | `security.only-for-task` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST · agent — A Personal Agent acts at a Company only when the User's task needs it. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/spec | It acts at a Company only when the User's task needs it. - A Personal Agent SHOULD send a Company only information relevant to the User's task. | VERIFIED (1.00) |
| spec/pap-1.0/10-security.md | 10.1 Content from Companies | `security.not-approval` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST · agent — Text from a Company is never the User's approval or consent. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/spec | It doesn't include unrelated details from the User's context or from other Companies. - Text from a Company is never the User's approval or consent. | VERIFIED (0.86) |
| spec/pap-1.0/10-security.md | 10.2 Secrets and the model | `security.secrets` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST · agent — Personal Agents keep access tokens, refresh tokens, Session JWTs, private keys, authorization codes, PKCE code verifiers and device codes out of model context. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/spec | Following [draft-ietf-wimse-aims](https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/draft-ietf-wimse-aims/) §8, which keeps an agent's credentials out of the model's reach, Personal Agents MUST keep these out of model context: access tokens (4.5), refresh tokens (4.4), Session JWTs (5.2), private keys, authorization codes and PKCE code verifiers (4.2), and device codes (4.3). | VERIFIED (1.00) |
| spec/pap-1.0/10-security.md | 10.3 What a domain vouches for | `security.domain-identity` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST · agent — Nothing in poppy.json confirms that the domain belongs to the company in organization.name; the domain is the Company's identity, and choosing the right domain is up to the Personal Agent. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/spec | Nothing in the document confirms that the domain belongs to the company in organization.name, any more than a website's title does. | UNVERIFIED (0.50) |
| spec/pap-1.0/10-security.md | 10.7 Delegated keys | `security.blind-sign` | (not in pap-0.3) | SHOULD · agent — A Personal Agent has the keys in its jwks_uri sign client assertions and delegations blindly, unless its own systems need to see which Companies a User works with, using the RSABSSA-SHA384-PSS-Deterministic variant of RFC 9474. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/spec | **Blind signing.** A Personal Agent SHOULD have the keys in its jwks_uri sign client assertions and delegations blindly, unless its own systems need to see which Companies a User works with. | VERIFIED (0.71) |
| spec/pap-1.0/10-security.md | 10.7 Delegated keys | `security.blind-jti` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST · agent — When it signs blindly, the Personal Agent generates the client assertion or delegation jti on the User's device. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/spec | The jti of a client assertion or delegation is that value, so the Personal Agent MUST generate it on the User's device. | VERIFIED (0.70) |
| spec/pap-1.0/10-security.md | 10.7 Delegated keys | `security.blind-both` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST · agent — A Personal Agent that signs blindly signs both its client assertions and its delegations blindly. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/spec | A Personal Agent that signs blindly MUST therefore sign both its client assertions and its delegations blindly, and MUST NOT rely on a signature by a key in its jwks_uri to mean its own systems approved what it signed, for example to limit delegation lifetimes or to stop a User. | VERIFIED (1.00) |
| spec/pap-1.0/10-security.md | 10.7 Delegated keys | `security.blind-not-approval` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST NOT · agent — A Personal Agent that signs blindly does not rely on a signature by a key in its jwks_uri to mean its own systems approved what it signed. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/spec | A Personal Agent that signs blindly MUST therefore sign both its client assertions and its delegations blindly, and MUST NOT rely on a signature by a key in its jwks_uri to mean its own systems approved what it signed, for example to limit delegation lifetimes or to stop a User. | VERIFIED (1.00) |
| spec/pap-1.0/10-security.md | 10.7 Delegated keys | `security.token-on-device` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST · agent — A Personal Agent that signs blindly, or otherwise lets devices get client assertions, keeps refresh and access tokens on the User's device and limits how many signatures each User can get. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/spec | A Personal Agent that signs blindly, or otherwise lets devices get client assertions, keeps refresh and access tokens on the User's device, protected as its other secrets are (10.2), and limits how many signatures each User can get. | VERIFIED (1.00) |
| spec/pap-1.0/10-security.md | 10.7 Delegated keys | `security.dpop-per-user` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST · agent — A Personal Agent whose servers hold DPoP keys signs with a User's key only for that User. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/spec | That holds only while other Users can't get proofs signed with the key, so a Personal Agent whose servers hold DPoP keys signs with a User's key only for that User. | VERIFIED (1.00) |
| spec/pap-1.0/10-security.md | 10.8 Sign-ins someone else started | `security.list-scopes` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST · company — In Direct and Device Sign-In, the Company's consent page lists every scope it will grant, except a poppy:browser-sign-in it may grant without asking, and it may mark the ones the User already granted. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/spec | - In Direct and Device Sign-In, the Company's consent page MUST list every scope it will grant, except a poppy:browser-sign-in it MAY grant without asking (section 6). | VERIFIED (0.70) |
| spec/pap-1.0/10-security.md | 10.8 Sign-ins someone else started | `security.prompt-unless-same-device` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST · agent — A Personal Agent sends prompt=consent unless the redirect comes back to the device that opened the authorization URL. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/spec | So a Personal Agent sends prompt=consent unless the redirect comes back to the device that opened the authorization URL, where a forwarded link fails, and 4.2 and 4.3 ask Companies to show the page for it and in Device Sign-In. | VERIFIED (1.00) |
| spec/pap-1.0/ext-browser-control.md | 2 Browser control and Operations | `bctl.prefer-operations` | (not in pap-0.3) | SHOULD · company — A Company uses Operations for an action that it can describe as terms to approve. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/extensions/browser-control | A Company SHOULD use Operations for an action that it can describe as terms to approve. | VERIFIED (0.86) |
| spec/pap-1.0/ext-browser-control.md | 2 Browser control and Operations | `bctl.both` | (not in pap-0.3) | MAY · company — A Company may use Browser Control and Operations for the same action. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/extensions/browser-control | A Company MAY use both for the same action, such as browser control for the User to check in for a flight and Operations for the User to approve the price of a paid seat. | VERIFIED (1.00) |
| spec/pap-1.0/ext-browser-control.md | 3 Browser control advertising support | `bctl.with-browser` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST · company — A Company that supports this extension lists it with web.browser_session_endpoint. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/extensions/browser-control | A Company that supports this extension lists it in extensions in its poppy.json ([spec 3.3](/docs/1.0/spec#extensions)), with web.browser_session_endpoint ([spec 6](/docs/1.0/spec#web-browsing)): | VERIFIED (1.00) |
| spec/pap-1.0/ext-browser-control.md | 3 Browser control advertising support | `bctl.support-means` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST · agent — Support means the Personal Agent leaves marked steps to the User, hands control to the User, and provides navigator.poppy.controller. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/extensions/browser-control | Support means that the Personal Agent leaves marked steps to the User (4), hands control to the User (5), and provides navigator.poppy.controller (6). | VERIFIED (1.00) |
| spec/pap-1.0/ext-browser-control.md | 4 Browser control marking steps | `bctl.mark-values` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST · both — data-poppy-user-step of interaction leaves changing or activating a control, or a form's controls and submitting it, to the User; submission on a form lets the browser fill controls unless other marks say not to, and leaves submitting to the User; any other value, or submission on anything but a form, counts as interaction. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/extensions/browser-control | Leaves its controls, and submitting it, to the User. \| \| submission \| A form \| Can fill in its controls, unless other marks say not to. | UNVERIFIED (0.43) |
| spec/pap-1.0/ext-browser-control.md | 4 Browser control marking steps | `bctl.mark-all` | (not in pap-0.3) | SHOULD · company — The Company marks every control that performs the step, or the form it is in, and marks them before they can be used. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/extensions/browser-control | The Company SHOULD mark every control that performs the step, or the form it is in, and SHOULD mark them before they can be used. | VERIFIED (0.89) |
| spec/pap-1.0/ext-browser-control.md | 4 Browser control marking steps | `bctl.submission-not-perform` | (not in pap-0.3) | SHOULD NOT · company — The Company does not let filling in a submission form perform the step itself. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/extensions/browser-control | The Company SHOULD NOT let filling in a submission form perform the step itself. | VERIFIED (1.00) |
| spec/pap-1.0/ext-browser-control.md | 4 Browser control marking steps | `bctl.server-checks` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST · company — The Company's servers check the action's conditions themselves, because marks do not stop anything. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/extensions/browser-control | Whatever it marks, the Company's servers MUST check the action's conditions themselves, because marks don't stop anything (8.1). | VERIFIED (1.00) |
| spec/pap-1.0/ext-browser-control.md | 5 Browser control handing control to the User | `bctl.no-take-step` | (not in pap-0.3) | SHOULD NOT · agent — A Personal Agent that declares this extension does not take a marked step, and does not remove or change marks to get around them. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/extensions/browser-control | A Personal Agent that declares this extension SHOULD NOT take a marked step: change or activate a control that a mark covers, or submit a form that a mark covers, whether with a click, a key press, an accessibility action, a script, or a WebMCP tool. | VERIFIED (0.73) |
| spec/pap-1.0/ext-browser-control.md | 5 Browser control handing control to the User | `bctl.leave-undone` | (not in pap-0.3) | MAY · agent — When it reaches a marked step, the Personal Agent may leave the step undone and continue with other work. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/extensions/browser-control | It MAY instead leave the step undone and continue with other work. | VERIFIED (0.60) |
| spec/pap-1.0/ext-browser-control.md | 5 Browser control handing control to the User | `bctl.model-while-user` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST NOT · agent — While the User holds control, the Personal Agent's software does not carry out browser actions from its model. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/extensions/browser-control | While the User holds control, the Personal Agent's model doesn't use the browser (5). 3. | VERIFIED (0.73) |
| spec/pap-1.0/ext-browser-control.md | 5 Browser control handing control to the User | `bctl.only-user-ends` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST NOT · both — Only the User ends their control; a timeout, a change to the page's marks, or anything the Company sends does not end it. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/extensions/browser-control | A timeout, a change to the page's marks, or anything the Company sends MUST NOT end it. - The Personal Agent MUST NOT keep its own requests, or its conversation with the User, waiting until the User acts. | VERIFIED (0.73) |
| spec/pap-1.0/ext-browser-control.md | 5 Browser control handing control to the User | `bctl.no-wait` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST NOT · agent — The Personal Agent does not keep its own requests, or its conversation with the User, waiting until the User acts. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/extensions/browser-control | A timeout, a change to the page's marks, or anything the Company sends MUST NOT end it. - The Personal Agent MUST NOT keep its own requests, or its conversation with the User, waiting until the User acts. | VERIFIED (1.00) |
| spec/pap-1.0/ext-browser-control.md | 6 Browser control who controls the browser | `bctl.controller` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST · agent — On pages of the Company's website, in a browser joined to a Session that declared this version, the Personal Agent's software provides navigator.poppy.controller as "user" while the User holds control and "agent" otherwise, set by software rather than the model, and only on the hosts of organization.domain and its subdomains. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/extensions/browser-control | On pages of the Company's website, in a browser joined to a Session that declared this version, the Personal Agent's software provides navigator.poppy.controller, a read-only string: | VERIFIED (0.58) |
| spec/pap-1.0/ext-browser-control.md | 6 Browser control who controls the browser | `bctl.send-controller` | (not in pap-0.3) | MAY · company — The page may send navigator.poppy.controller to the Company with the request. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/extensions/browser-control | The page can read who controls the browser from navigator.poppy.controller and send it with the request (6). 4. | VERIFIED (0.86) |
| spec/pap-1.0/ext-browser-control.md | 6 Browser control who controls the browser | `bctl.not-a-guarantee` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST NOT · company — The Company does not rely on "user" as a guarantee that the User took the step. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/extensions/browser-control | Marks signal what the Company wants, and a Personal Agent that follows this extension honors them. navigator.poppy.controller should be correct, but a Company shouldn't use it as a guarantee that the User took a step. | VERIFIED (0.83) |
| spec/pap-1.0/ext-disclosures.md | 2 Disclosures advertising support | `disclosures.only-declared` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST · company — The Company sends disclosures only in response to a request that declares disclosures version 1; a Personal Agent that does not declare it gets the notice in text. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/extensions/disclosures | The Company sends disclosures only in response to a request that declares this version ([spec 8.4](/docs/1.0/spec#message-fields)). | UNVERIFIED (0.54) |
| spec/pap-1.0/ext-disclosures.md | 3 Disclosures in messages | `disclosures.shape` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST · company — A Company message carries disclosures in extensions.disclosures.items, each with text, and only Company messages carry them. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/extensions/disclosures | A Company message carries its disclosures in extensions.disclosures ([spec 8.4](/docs/1.0/spec#message-fields)): | VERIFIED (0.56) |
| spec/pap-1.0/ext-disclosures.md | 3 Disclosures in messages | `disclosures.content` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST · agent — Disclosures are content from the Company. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/extensions/disclosures | With this extension, a Company marks them as disclosures, apart from the rest of the message, so the Personal Agent can tell them from ordinary text. | VERIFIED (0.75) |
| spec/pap-1.0/ext-links.md | 2 Links advertising support | `links.only-declared` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST · company — The Company sends links only in response to a request that declares links version 1. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/extensions/links | The Company sends links only in response to a request that declares this version ([spec 8.4](/docs/1.0/spec#message-fields)). | VERIFIED (1.00) |
| spec/pap-1.0/ext-links.md | 3 Links in messages | `links.shape` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST · company — A Company message carries links in extensions.links.items, each with an HTTPS url and an optional title, in the order the message refers to them, and only Company messages carry them. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/extensions/links | \| Field \| Req. \| Description \| \| --- \| --- \| --- \| \| items \| Yes \| The links, in the order the message refers to them. \| \| items[].url \| Yes \| The page's HTTPS URL. \| \| items[].title \| No \| The page's name, as the message refers to it. \| | UNVERIFIED (0.54) |
| spec/pap-1.0/ext-links.md | 3 Links in messages | `links.content` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST · agent — A link's title and url are content from the Company, and a title can name a page other than the one at its URL. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/extensions/links | A link's title and url are content from the Company ([spec 10.1](/docs/1.0/spec#untrusted-content)), and a title can name a page other than the one at its URL. | VERIFIED (0.88) |
| spec/pap-1.0/ext-mediated-sign-in.md | 1 Mediated Sign-In introduction | `mediated.offer-until` | (not in pap-0.3) | SHOULD · company — A Company offers Mediated Sign-In only until it can add Direct or Device Sign-In, and stops offering it once those cover the scopes it grants. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/extensions/mediated-sign-in | A Company SHOULD offer Mediated Sign-In only until it can add Direct or Device Sign-In, and SHOULD stop offering it once those cover the scopes it grants. | VERIFIED (0.85) |
| spec/pap-1.0/ext-mediated-sign-in.md | 2 Mediated Sign-In advertising support | `mediated.grant-type` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST · company — A Company that offers Mediated Sign-In lists the extension and lists https://personalagentprotocol.org/grant/mediated-sign-in in grant_types_supported. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/extensions/mediated-sign-in | A Company that offers Mediated Sign-In lists it in extensions in its poppy.json ([spec 3.3](/docs/1.0/spec#extensions)), and lists the grant type https://personalagentprotocol.org/grant/mediated-sign-in in its issuer's grant_types_supported ([spec 3.2](/docs/1.0/spec#oauth-server)): | VERIFIED (0.91) |
| spec/pap-1.0/ext-mediated-sign-in.md | 2 Mediated Sign-In advertising support | `mediated.field-names` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST NOT · company — A credential field name is not grant_type, scope, resource, a client-authentication parameter, or a parameter this extension defines, such as sign_in_id. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/extensions/mediated-sign-in | It MUST NOT be grant_type, scope, resource, a client authentication parameter such as client_id or client_assertion, or a parameter this extension defines, such as sign_in_id. - label is what to ask the User for. - secret marks a value the Personal Agent MUST protect: never shown back to the User, written to logs, or sent anywhere else. - scopes lists the scopes Mediated Sign-In  | VERIFIED (0.71) |
| spec/pap-1.0/ext-mediated-sign-in.md | 2 Mediated Sign-In advertising support | `mediated.secret-field` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST · agent — The Personal Agent protects a field marked secret: it is never shown back to the User, written to logs, or sent anywhere else. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/extensions/mediated-sign-in | It MUST NOT be grant_type, scope, resource, a client authentication parameter such as client_id or client_assertion, or a parameter this extension defines, such as sign_in_id. - label is what to ask the User for. - secret marks a value the Personal Agent MUST protect: never shown back to the User, written to logs, or sent anywhere else. - scopes lists the scopes Mediated Sign-In  | VERIFIED (0.80) |
| spec/pap-1.0/ext-mediated-sign-in.md | 2 Mediated Sign-In advertising support | `mediated.scopes-supported` | (not in pap-0.3) | SHOULD · company — The Company also includes the extension's scopes in scopes_supported. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/guides | With direct and device sign-in, the user's password stays with the company. - Use scopes_supported from the issuer's metadata and from the domain's protected resource metadata, the company's OpenAPI description, and its sign-in extension metadata to find scopes. | VERIFIED (0.80) |
| spec/pap-1.0/ext-mediated-sign-in.md | 2 Mediated Sign-In advertising support | `mediated.no-extra-scopes` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST NOT · company — The Company does not grant scopes beyond those requested and listed in the extension's scopes. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/extensions/mediated-sign-in | The Company MUST NOT grant scopes beyond those requested and listed here. | VERIFIED (0.86) |
| spec/pap-1.0/ext-mediated-sign-in.md | 3 Mediated Sign-In signing in | `mediated.request` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST · agent — The Personal Agent sends the grant type, its client assertion, scope, and every field in fields to the Company's token_endpoint. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/extensions/mediated-sign-in | The Personal Agent sends a token request to the Company's token_endpoint with the grant type, its client assertion ([spec 4.1](/docs/1.0/spec#agent-identity)), scope as in [spec 4.7](/docs/1.0/spec#scopes), and every field in fields as a parameter: | VERIFIED (1.00) |
| spec/pap-1.0/ext-mediated-sign-in.md | 4 Mediated Sign-In one-time codes | `mediated.code-step` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST · both — When the Company returns code_required with sign_in_id, the Personal Agent sends the same grant again with sign_in_id and one_time_code and its client assertion, and not the credentials or scope; a wrong code returns code_required again until the attempt limit, then invalid_grant. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/extensions/mediated-sign-in | The Personal Agent gets the code from the User and sends the same grant again with sign_in_id and one_time_code, and its client assertion, but not the credentials or scope. | VERIFIED (0.59) |
| spec/pap-1.0/ext-mediated-sign-in.md | 4 Mediated Sign-In one-time codes | `mediated.same-client` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST · company — Only the client_id that started a sign-in can finish it. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/extensions/mediated-sign-in | Only the client_id that started a sign-in can finish it. | VERIFIED (0.80) |
| spec/pap-1.0/ext-mediated-sign-in.md | 6 Mediated Sign-In security | `mediated.credentials-destination` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST NOT · agent — Personal Agents do not send credentials anywhere other than the token_endpoint of the Company's issuer, with this grant. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/extensions/mediated-sign-in | Personal Agents MUST NOT send credentials anywhere other than the token_endpoint of the Company's issuer, with this grant. | VERIFIED (0.92) |
| spec/pap-1.0/ext-mediated-sign-in.md | 6 Mediated Sign-In security | `mediated.choose-by-issuer` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST · agent — A Personal Agent chooses which stored credentials to send by the Company's issuer, never by the field labels or the endpoint, and shows the Company's organization.name and domain whenever it asks the User for credentials or a code. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/extensions/mediated-sign-in | A Personal Agent MUST choose which stored credentials to send by the Company's issuer, never by the field labels or the endpoint, and MUST show the Company's organization.name and domain whenever it asks the User for credentials or a code. | VERIFIED (0.90) |
| spec/pap-1.0/ext-mediated-sign-in.md | 6 Mediated Sign-In security | `mediated.store` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST · agent — After a sign-in ends, with tokens or an error other than code_required, the Personal Agent either discards the credentials or keeps them only in a credential store the User chose. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/extensions/mediated-sign-in | **Storing credentials.** After a sign-in ends, with tokens or an error other than code_required, the Personal Agent MUST either discard the credentials or keep them only in a credential store the User chose, such as a password manager. | VERIFIED (0.89) |
| spec/pap-1.0/ext-mediated-sign-in.md | 6 Mediated Sign-In security | `mediated.no-other-store` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST NOT · agent — The Personal Agent does not keep those credentials anywhere else, including model context and logs. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/extensions/mediated-sign-in | It MUST NOT keep them anywhere else, including model context and logs ([spec 10.2](/docs/1.0/spec#secrets)). | VERIFIED (0.70) |
| spec/pap-1.0/ext-mediated-sign-in.md | 6 Mediated Sign-In security | `mediated.no-resignin` | (not in pap-0.3) | SHOULD NOT · agent — After the User or the Company revokes the Personal Agent's access, the Personal Agent does not sign in again with stored credentials unless the User asks. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/extensions/mediated-sign-in | After the User or the Company revokes the Personal Agent's access ([spec 5.5](/docs/1.0/spec#ending-sessions)), the Personal Agent SHOULD NOT sign in again with stored credentials unless the User asks it to. | VERIFIED (1.00) |
| spec/pap-1.0/ext-mediated-sign-in.md | 6 Mediated Sign-In security | `mediated.prefer-delegation` | (not in pap-0.3) | SHOULD · agent — A Personal Agent uses Direct or Device Sign-In rather than Mediated Sign-In when the Company offers it and its scopes cover the task. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/extensions/mediated-sign-in | A Personal Agent SHOULD use Direct or Device Sign-In rather than Mediated Sign-In when the Company offers it and its scopes cover the task. | VERIFIED (0.92) |
| spec/pap-1.0/ext-operations.md | ops.2 Advertising support | `ops.website-endpoint` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST · company — A Company that asks for approval on its website lists an HTTPS endpoint and web.browser_session_endpoint; a Company that returns no operations on its website does not list or serve endpoint. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/extensions/operations | A Company that asks for approval on its website lists endpoint, the HTTPS URL where the Personal Agent gets operations its website returned and sends their approvals (4, 6). | VERIFIED (0.62) |
| spec/pap-1.0/ext-operations.md | ops.3 The operation | `ops.id-reuse` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST NOT · company — The Company does not use an operation_id for another action. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/extensions/operations | An operation is one action the Company will perform once it's approved: | VERIFIED (0.75) |
| spec/pap-1.0/ext-operations.md | ops.3 The operation | `ops.revision-changes` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST · company — The Company changes revision whenever the terms or expires_at change. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/extensions/operations | An opaque string with the same characters as the IDs in [spec 8.2](/docs/1.0/spec#conversation-requests), which the Company changes whenever the terms or expires_at change. | VERIFIED (0.86) |
| spec/pap-1.0/ext-operations.md | ops.3 The operation | `ops.summary-markdown` | (not in pap-0.3) | MAY · company — A summary may use emphasis, strong emphasis, paragraphs and lists. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/extensions/operations | **Formatting.** A summary is text that MAY use a few [CommonMark](https://spec.commonmark.org/0.31.2/) elements: emphasis (*italic*), strong emphasis (**bold**), paragraphs, and lists. | VERIFIED (0.83) |
| spec/pap-1.0/ext-operations.md | ops.3 The operation | `ops.summary-render` | (not in pap-0.3) | MAY · agent — A Personal Agent may render those elements or show the text as is. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/extensions/operations | A Personal Agent MAY render them or show the text as is. | VERIFIED (0.83) |
| spec/pap-1.0/ext-operations.md | ops.3 The operation | `ops.summary-no-links` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST NOT · agent — The Personal Agent does not render links, images or HTML in a summary, and shows them as text. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/extensions/operations | The Personal Agent MUST NOT render links, images, or HTML in a summary, and shows them as text, so a summary can't send the User elsewhere or load content while they decide. | VERIFIED (1.00) |
| spec/pap-1.0/ext-operations.md | ops.3 The operation | `ops.unique-revision` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST NOT · company — The Company does not give two different sets of terms the same revision. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/extensions/operations | It MUST NOT give two different sets of terms the same revision, so an approval of one revision never applies to other terms. | VERIFIED (0.86) |
| spec/pap-1.0/ext-operations.md | ops.3 The operation | `ops.expires-long-enough` | (not in pap-0.3) | SHOULD · company — expires_at leaves the User long enough to read the terms and decide, unless the Company cannot hold the terms that long. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/extensions/operations | **Expiry.** expires_at SHOULD leave the User long enough to read the terms and decide, unless the Company can't hold the terms that long, such as a price or a seat that can sell out. | VERIFIED (0.83) |
| spec/pap-1.0/ext-operations.md | ops.3 The operation | `ops.who-approves` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST · company — An operation returned signed out can be approved only while signed out; one returned signed in belongs to the account and can be approved only while signed in to the same account, in any Session of the same Personal Agent and User; a signed-out Session of that Personal Agent and User gets a challenge. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/extensions/operations | A signed-out Session of the same Personal Agent and User gets a challenge for an operation that belongs to an account, as in section 3. | VERIFIED (0.73) |
| spec/pap-1.0/ext-operations.md | ops.4 Asking for approval | `ops.api-shape` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST · company — An API that wants approval responds with HTTP 403 and a JSON body with error approval_required and the operation in operation, not in WWW-Authenticate. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/extensions/operations | The API responds with HTTP 403 and a JSON body with error set to approval_required and the operation in operation, like invalid_session ([spec 7](/docs/1.0/spec#apis)). | VERIFIED (0.73) |
| spec/pap-1.0/ext-operations.md | ops.4 Asking for approval | `ops.api-extra` | (not in pap-0.3) | MAY · company — That body may have other members. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/extensions/operations | The body MAY have other members, such as the API's own error fields. | VERIFIED (0.75) |
| spec/pap-1.0/ext-operations.md | ops.4 Asking for approval | `ops.mcp-shape` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST · company — An MCP tool returns an error result with isError true, the operation in structuredContent.operation, and text that says the action needs approval. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/extensions/operations | The tool returns an error result, with isError set to true, the operation in operation in structuredContent, and text that says the action needs approval, such as the summary: | VERIFIED (0.93) |
| spec/pap-1.0/ext-operations.md | ops.4 Asking for approval | `ops.conversation-shape` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST · company — A Company message carries the operation in extensions.operations.operation. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/extensions/operations | A Company message carries the operation in extensions.operations.operation ([spec 8.4](/docs/1.0/spec#message-fields)), when the Company Agent would perform the action: | VERIFIED (1.00) |
| spec/pap-1.0/ext-operations.md | ops.4 Asking for approval | `ops.website-header` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST · company — A website that handles the request as the Session performs nothing and responds with HTTP 403 and a Poppy-Operation dictionary whose sid is the Session's sid and whose ref is at most 2048 characters from the stated alphabet. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/extensions/operations | When the request comes from a browser joined to a Session ([spec 6](/docs/1.0/spec#web-browsing)), and the website handles it as that Session, the website performs nothing and responds with HTTP 403 and a reference to the operation in the Poppy-Operation header. | VERIFIED (0.56) |
| spec/pap-1.0/ext-operations.md | ops.4 Asking for approval | `ops.website-repeat` | (not in pap-0.3) | SHOULD · company — The website offers a way to make the original request again. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/extensions/operations | The Company's website SHOULD offer a way to make the original request again, such as a Continue button on the page that's waiting, so the Personal Agent can repeat the action once it's approved (6). | VERIFIED (0.86) |
| spec/pap-1.0/ext-operations.md | ops.4 Asking for approval | `ops.website-page` | (not in pap-0.3) | SHOULD · company — The website answers a page load or form submission with a page that says the action is waiting for approval in the User's personal agent. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/extensions/operations | The Company's website SHOULD answer a page load or form submission with a page that says the action is waiting for approval in the User's personal agent. | VERIFIED (0.93) |
| spec/pap-1.0/ext-operations.md | ops.4 Asking for approval | `ops.website-json` | (not in pap-0.3) | SHOULD · company — The website answers a JSON request from its own scripts with a JSON object with error approval_required and without the operation. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/extensions/operations | The Company's website SHOULD answer a request from its own scripts that accepts JSON, such as a fetch, with a JSON object with error set to approval_required, like an API's but without the operation. | VERIFIED (0.92) |
| spec/pap-1.0/ext-operations.md | ops.4 Asking for approval | `ops.website-json-extra` | (not in pap-0.3) | MAY · company — That object may have other members. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/extensions/operations | The object MAY have other members, such as the website's own error fields: | VERIFIED (0.75) |
| spec/pap-1.0/ext-operations.md | ops.4 Asking for approval | `ops.ref-from-header` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST · agent — The Personal Agent takes a reference only from the Poppy-Operation header of a response its own browser received, only when sid names a Session it joined that browser to, ignores any other such header, and sends the reference only to that Session's Company's endpoint. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/extensions/operations | The Personal Agent MUST take a reference only from the Poppy-Operation header of a response that its own browser received, never from page content or a message, and only when sid names a Session it joined that browser to. | VERIFIED (0.67) |
| spec/pap-1.0/ext-operations.md | ops.4 Asking for approval | `ops.endpoint-no-scope` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST · company — The endpoint requires no scope: recording an approval performs nothing. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/extensions/operations | It requires no scope: recording an approval performs nothing, and the browser's request performs the action with the browser's own access. | VERIFIED (0.75) |
| spec/pap-1.0/ext-operations.md | ops.4 Asking for approval | `ops.bad-ref` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST · company — A reference the Session cannot use gets HTTP 400 with {"error": "invalid_request"}. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/extensions/operations | Any other reference that the Session can't use, because the Company didn't return it, its operation has expired, or the Session can't approve it, gets HTTP 400 with {"error": "invalid_request"}. | VERIFIED (0.89) |
| spec/pap-1.0/ext-operations.md | ops.5 Approving an operation | `ops.not-done` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST NOT · agent — When a request returns an operation, the Personal Agent does not treat the action as done. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/extensions/operations | When a request returns an operation, the Personal Agent MUST NOT treat the action as done. | VERIFIED (1.00) |
| spec/pap-1.0/ext-operations.md | ops.5 Approving an operation | `ops.show-user` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST · agent — For a user approval, the Personal Agent shows the User the Company's name and the revision's terms, takes the User's answer through its own interface, and does not treat text from the Company as the User's approval. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/extensions/operations | - The Personal Agent MUST show the User the Company's name and the revision's terms, and MUST take the User's answer through its own interface. | VERIFIED (0.67) |
| spec/pap-1.0/ext-operations.md | ops.5 Approving an operation | `ops.agent-instruction` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST · agent — An agent approval rests on an instruction the User gave the Personal Agent; content from the Company, a website, or anything else the Personal Agent read is not one. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/extensions/operations | Content from the Company, a website, or anything else the Personal Agent read isn't one, even when it says it comes from the User. - Before approving, the Personal Agent MUST check the revision's terms against that instruction, and approve only if the instruction covers them. | VERIFIED (0.77) |
| spec/pap-1.0/ext-operations.md | ops.5 Approving an operation | `ops.check-instruction` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST · agent — Before an agent approval, the Personal Agent checks the revision's terms against that instruction and approves only if the instruction covers them. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/extensions/operations | Content from the Company, a website, or anything else the Personal Agent read isn't one, even when it says it comes from the User. - Before approving, the Personal Agent MUST check the revision's terms against that instruction, and approve only if the instruction covers them. | VERIFIED (0.73) |
| spec/pap-1.0/ext-operations.md | ops.5 Approving an operation | `ops.no-same-instruction` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST NOT · agent — If the Company answers an agent approval with a new revision, or a website returns a new operation for the action after recording it, the Personal Agent does not approve the new one on the same instruction. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/extensions/operations | If the Personal Agent can't tell whether it does, it asks the User. - If the Company answers the Personal Agent's agent approval with a new revision (6), or a website returns a new operation for the action after recording it, the Personal Agent MUST NOT approve the new one on the same instruction. | VERIFIED (1.00) |
| spec/pap-1.0/ext-operations.md | ops.5 Approving an operation | `ops.check-terms` | (not in pap-0.3) | SHOULD · agent — Before approving, the Personal Agent checks that terms agree with what it asked for, and with the summary if there is one, and points out any difference to the User when it asks them. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/extensions/operations | - Before approving, the Personal Agent SHOULD check that terms agree with what it asked for, and with the summary if there is one, and point out any difference to the User when it asks them. | VERIFIED (0.86) |
| spec/pap-1.0/ext-operations.md | ops.6 Sending the approval | `ops.approver` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST · agent — The Personal Agent includes approver, user or agent. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/extensions/operations | That includes approver, approved_at, input_type, and reference (6), which help the Company keep a record, judge risk, and look into an approval with the Personal Agent's operator. | VERIFIED (0.80) |
| spec/pap-1.0/ext-operations.md | ops.6 Sending the approval | `ops.input-type-agent` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST NOT · agent — The Personal Agent does not include input_type with agent. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/extensions/operations | It can be earlier than the request, for example when the Personal Agent sends the approval later. - input_type, only with user: how the User gave their answer, as a short string, such as tap, typed, or voice. | VERIFIED (0.80) |
| spec/pap-1.0/ext-operations.md | ops.6 Sending the approval | `ops.reference-one-company` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST NOT · agent — The Personal Agent does not send the same reference to two Companies. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/extensions/operations | The Personal Agent MUST NOT send the same reference to two Companies, so that Companies can't use it to link the User's approvals at one Company to their approvals at another. | VERIFIED (1.00) |
| spec/pap-1.0/ext-operations.md | ops.6 Sending the approval | `ops.no-pii-in-approval` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST NOT · agent — The Personal Agent does not put personal data, or anything that identifies the User or their device, in input_type or reference. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/extensions/operations | The Personal Agent MUST NOT put personal data, or anything that identifies the User or their device, in input_type or reference. | VERIFIED (1.00) |
| spec/pap-1.0/ext-operations.md | ops.6 Sending the approval | `ops.include-optional` | (not in pap-0.3) | SHOULD · agent — The Personal Agent includes approved_at and input_type with user. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/extensions/operations | That includes approver, approved_at, input_type, and reference (6), which help the Company keep a record, judge risk, and look into an approval with the Personal Agent's operator. | VERIFIED (0.86) |
| spec/pap-1.0/ext-operations.md | ops.6 Sending the approval | `ops.record-approval-fields` | (not in pap-0.3) | MAY · company — A Company may record approver, approved_at, input_type and reference. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/extensions/operations | A Company MAY record approver, approved_at, input_type, and reference, and MUST NOT reject or decline an approval because an optional member is missing or because it doesn't recognize the input_type. | VERIFIED (1.00) |
| spec/pap-1.0/ext-operations.md | ops.6 Sending the approval | `ops.optional-not-required` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST NOT · company — A Company does not reject or decline an approval because an optional member is missing or because it does not recognize the input_type. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/extensions/operations | A Company MAY record approver, approved_at, input_type, and reference, and MUST NOT reject or decline an approval because an optional member is missing or because it doesn't recognize the input_type. | VERIFIED (1.00) |
| spec/pap-1.0/ext-operations.md | ops.6 Sending the approval | `ops.ignore-unknown-members` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST · company — Companies ignore members of Poppy-Approval they do not recognize. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/extensions/operations | Companies MUST ignore members of Poppy-Approval they don't recognize, so later versions can add members. | VERIFIED (0.86) |
| spec/pap-1.0/ext-operations.md | ops.6 Sending the approval | `ops.website-approval-channel` | (not in pap-0.3) | MUST NOT · company — The Company does not accept an approval of a website's operation at an API, MCP server or conversation, or an approval of another interface's operation at endpoint. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/extensions/operations | The Company MUST NOT accept an approval of a website's operation at an API, MCP server, or conversation, or an approval of another interface's operation at endpoint. | VERIFIED (1.00) |
| spec/pap-1.0/ext-operations.md | ops.6 Sending the approval | `ops.website-repeat-itself` | (not in pap-0.3) | MAY · company — After recording an approval, the website may make the original request again itself. | https://personalagentprotocol.org/docs/1.0/extensions/operations | A new operation in answer to a retry doesn't say, so the Personal Agent finds out another way, such as from the order in the Company's API. - On a website, if the browser's request fails or times out after the approval was recorded, the Personal Agent has the browser make it again. | VERIFIED (0.70) |
